Ok(Cli::try_parse()?)\n696\t}\n697\t\n698\tpub fn run(cli: Cli) -> Result<()> {\n699\t    let workspace = cli.workspace.unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(\".\"));\n700\t\n701\t    // For init command, we allow the .beads directory to not exist yet\n702\t    match &cli.command {\n703\t        Commands::Init { .. } => {\n704\t            let beads_dir = workspace.join(\".beads\");\n705\t            return match cli.command {\n706\t                Commands::Init { prefix } => cmd_init(&beads_dir, &prefix),\n707\t                _ => unreachable!(),\n708\t            };\n709\t        }\n710\t        _ => {}\n711\t    }\n712\t\n713\t    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(&workspace)\n714\t        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace))?;\n715\t\n716\t    match cli.command {\n717\t        Commands::Init { prefix } => cmd_init(&beads_dir, &prefix),\n718\t        Commands::Create {\n719\t            title,\n720\t            type_,\n721\t            priority,\n722\t            description,\n723\t            assignee,\n724\t            label,\n725\t        } => cmd_create(\n726\t            &beads_dir,\n727\t            title,\n728\t            type_,\n729\t            priority,\n730\t            description,\n731\t            assignee,\n732\t            label,\n733\t        ),\n734\t        Commands::List {\n735\t            status,\n736\t            type_,\n737\t            assignee,\n738\t            priority,\n739\t            annotation,\n740\t            limit,\n741\t            all,\n742\t            format,\n743\t            json,\n744\t        } => {\n745\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n746\t            cmd_list(\n747\t                &beads_dir, status, type_, assignee, priority, annotation, limit, all, &format,\n748\t            )\n749\t        }\n750\t        Commands::Show { id, format, json } => {\n751\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n752\t            cmd_show(&beads_dir, &id, &format)\n753\t        }\n754\t        Commands::Update {\n755\t            id,\n756\t            title,\n757\t            status,\n758\t            priority,\n759\t            assignee,\n760\t            description,\n761\t            acceptance_criteria,\n762\t            notes,\n763\t            design,\n764\t            due_at,\n765\t        } => cmd_update(\n766\t            &beads_dir,\n767\t            &id,\n768\t            title,\n769\t            status,\n770\t            priority,\n771\t            assignee,\n772\t            description,\n773\t            acceptance_criteria,\n774\t            notes,\n775\t            design,\n776\t            due_at,\n777\t        ),\n778\t        Commands::Close { id, reason } => cmd_close(&beads_dir, &id, &reason),\n779\t        Commands::Reopen { id } => cmd_reopen(&beads_dir, &id),\n780\t        Commands::Delete { id } => cmd_delete(&beads_dir, &id),\n781\t        Commands::Ready {\n782\t            limit,\n783\t            format,\n784\t            json,\n785\t        } => {\n786\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n787\t            cmd_ready(&beads_dir, limit, &format)\n788\t        }\n789\t        Commands::Claim {\n790\t            assignee,\n791\t            model,\n792\t            harness,\n793\t            harness_version,\n794\t            any,\n795\t            fallback,\n796\t            workspace_paths,\n797\t            dry_run,\n798\t            format,\n799\t            json,\n800\t        } => {\n801\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n802\t            cmd_claim(\n803\t                &beads_dir,\n804\t                &assignee,\n805\t                model,\n806\t                harness,\n807\t                harness_version,\n808\t                any,\n809\t                fallback.as_deref(),\n810\t                &workspace_paths,\n811\t                dry_run,\n812\t                &format,\n813\t            )\n814\t        }\n815\t        Commands::Sync {\n816\t            flush_only,\n817\t            import_only,\n818\t        } => Commands::Comments(comments) => cmd_comments(&beads_dir, comments),\n838\t        Commands::Search {\n839\t            query,\n840\t            status,\n841\t            type_,\n842\t   by_assignee,\n871\t            by_label,\n872\t            &format,\n873\t        ),\n874\t        Commands::Schema { target, format } => cmd_schema(&target, &format),\n875\t        Commands::Config(config) => cmd_config(&beads_dir, config),\n876\t        Commands::Velocity {\n877\t            model,\n878\t            harness,\n879\t            format,\n880\t        } => cmd_velocity(&beads_dir, model, harness, &format),\n881\t        Commands::Labels { id, format } => cmd_labels(&beads_dir, &id, &format),\n882\t        Commands::Annotate(annotate) => cmd_annotate(&beads_dir, annotate),\n883\t        Commands::Log {\n884\t            id,\n885\t            limit,\n886\t            since,\n887\t            actor,\n888\t            status_changes,\n889\t            diff,\n890\t            git,\n891\t            format,\n892\t            json,\n893\t        } => {\n894\t            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() }             status_changes,\n902\t                diff,\n903\t                git,\n904\t                &format,\n905\t            )\n906\t        }\n907\t        Commands::CriticalPath {\n908\t cmd_create(\n965\t    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n966\t    title: String,\n967\t    type_: String,\n968\t    priority: i32,\n969\t    description: Option<String>,\n970\t    assignee: Option<String>,\n971\t IssueType::from_str(type_.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n984\t    issue.priority = Priority(priority);\n985\t    issue.description = description;\n986\t    issue.assignee = beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n997\t    status: Option<String>,\n998\t    type_: Option<String>,\n999\t    assignee: Option<String>,\n1000\t    priority: Option<i32>,\n1001\t    annotation: Option<String>,\n1002\t    limit: Option<usize>,\n1003\t    all: bool,\n1004\t    format: &str,\n1005\t) -> Result<()> {\n1006\t    // Parse annotation filter (key=value format)\n1007\t    let annotation_filter = match annotation {\n1008\t        Some(ref ann) => {\n1009\t            let parts: Vec<&str> = ann.splitn(2, '=').collect();\n1010\t            if parts.len() != 2 {\n1011\t                return Err(anyhow!(\"Invalid annotation format. Use key=value\"));\n1012\t            }\n1013\t            Some((parts[0].to_string(), parts[1].to_string()))\n1014\t        }\n1015\t        None => None,\n1016\t    };\n1017\t\n1018\t    let {\n1023\t        // Include archived beads\n1024\t        list_all_with_archives(beads_dir)?\n1025\t    } else {\n1026\t        // Only from database\n1027\t        let mut filter = IssueFilter::default();\n1028\t        if let Some(ref s) = status {\n1029\t            filter.status = Some(Status::from_str(s.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?);\n1030\t        }\n1031\t        if let Some(ref t) = type_ {\n1032\t            filter.issue_type =\n1033\t                Some(IssueType::from_str(t.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?);\n1034\t        }\n1035\t        filter.assignee = assignee.clone();\n1036\t        filter.priority = priority;\n1037\t        filter.annotation = annotation_filter.clone();\n1038\t        // --limit 0 means unlimited\n1039\t        filter.limit = limit.and_then(|l| if l == 0 { None } else { Some(l) });\n1040\t        storage.list_issues(&filter)?\n1041\t    };\n1042\t\n1043\t    // Apply additional filters for --all mode (since we're not using DB filters)\n1044\t    if all {\n1045\t        if let Some(ref s) = status {\n1046\t            let status_filter = Status::from_str(s.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n1047\t            issues.retain(|i| i.status == status_filter);\n1048\t        }\n1049\t        if let Some(ref t) = type_ {\n1050\t            let type_filter = IssueType::from_str(t.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n1051\t            issues.retain(|i| i.issue_type == type_filter);\n1052\t        }\n1053\t        if let Some(ref assignee_val) = assignee {\n1054\t            issues.retain(|i| i.assignee.as_deref() == Some(assignee_val));\n1055\t        }\n1056\t        if let Some(p) = priority {\n1057\t            issues.retain(|i| i.priority.0 == p);\n1058\t        }\n1059\t        if let Some((ref key, ref value)) = annotation_filter {\n1060\t            issues.retain(|i| {\n1061\t                i.annotations\n1062\t                    .get(key)\n1063\t                    .map_or(false, |v| v == value)\n1064\t            });\n1065\t        }\n1066\t        // Apply limit\n1067\t        if let Some(l) = limit {\n1068\t            if l != 0 {\n1069\t                issues.truncate(l);\n1070\t            }\n1071\t        }\n1072\t    }\n1073\t\n1074\t    let output_format = OutputFormat::from_str(format).unwrap_or(OutputFormat::Text);\n1075\t    let formatter = get_formatter(output_format);\n1076\t    print!(\"{}\", formatter.format_issues(&issues));\n1077\t\n1078\t    Ok(())\n1079\t}\n1080\t\n1081\tfn cmd_show(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, format: &str) -> Result<()> {\n1082\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n1083\t    let db_path = println!(\"ID: {}\", issue.id);\n1109\t            println!(\"Title: {}\", issue.title);\n1110\t            println!(\"Status: {}\", issue.status);\n1111\t            println!(\"Priority: {}\", {}\", issue.id);\n1125\t            println!(\"Title: {}\", issue.title);\n1126\t            println!(\"Status: {}\", issue.status);\n1127\t            println!(\"Priority: {}\", issue.priority);\n1128\t            println!(\"Type: {}\", issue.issue_type);\n1129\t            if let Some(desc) = &issue.description {\n1130\t                println!(\"Description: {}\", desc);\n1131\t            }\n1132\t            if let Some(assignee) = &issue.assignee {\n1133\t                println!(\"Assignee: {}\", assignee);\n1134\t            }\n1135\t            if }\n1329\t\n1330\t            // Sort by score and take top 1\n1331\t            all_candidates.sort_by(|a, b| {\n1332\t                let score_a = (b.1.downstream_impact, b.1.priority, &[crate::storage::DepTreeNode], _storage: &Storage) -> Result<()> {\n1783\t    if nodes.is_empty() {\n1784\t        println!(\"  (no dependencies)\");\n1785\t        return Ok(());\n1786\t    }\n1787\t\n1788\t    // Status indicators: ●=open, ◐=in_progress, ○=closed/blocked/deferred, ⊘=tombstone\n1789\t    let status_symbol = |status: &str| -> char {\n1790\t        match status {\n1791\t            \"open\" => '●',\n1792\t            \"in_progress\" => '◐',\n1793\t            \"closed\" => '○',\n1794\t            \"blocked\" => '◌',\n1795\t            \"deferred\" => '○',\n1796\t            \"tombstone\" => '⊘',\n1797\t            _ => '○',\n1798\t        }\n1799\t    };\n1800\t\n1801\t    for (i, node) in nodes.iter().enumerate() {\n1802\t        let is_cycle = node.path.contains(\"[CYCLE]\");\n1803\t\n1804\t        // Build tree prefix with proper branching\n1805\t        let mut prefix = String::new();\n1806\t        if node.depth > 0 {\n1807\t            for d in 0..node.depth as usize {\n1808\t                if d < node.depth as usize - 1 {\n1809\t                    prefix.push_str(\"│   \");\n1810\t                } else {\n1811\t                    // Check if this is the last node at this depth\n1812\t                    let is_last = nodes\n1813\t                        .iter()\n1814\t                        .skip(i + 1)\n1815\t                        .all(|n| n.depth < node.depth || (n.depth == node.depth && n.id > node.id));\n1816\t                    if is_last {\n1817\t                        prefix.push_str(\"└── \");\n1818\t                    } else {\n1819\t                        prefix.push_str(\"├── \");\n1820\t                    }\n1821\t                }\n1822\t            }\n1823\t        }\n1824\t\n1825\t        // Truncate title if too long\n1826\t        let title = if node.title.len() > 60 {\n1827\t            format!(\"{}...\", &node.title[..57])\n1828\t        } else {\n1829\t            node.title.clone()\n1830\t        };\n1831\t\n1832\t        let cycle_mark = if is_cycle { \" [CYCLE]\" } else { \"\" };\n1833\t        let dep_type_str = node.dep_type.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"blocks\");\n1834\t\n1835\t        println!(\n1836\t            \"{}[{}] {} {} (P{}, {}){}\",\n1837\t            prefix,\n1838\t            node.id,\n1839\t }\n1846\t\n1847\t    Ok(())\n1848\t}\n1849\t\n1850\tfn cmd_dep(beads_dir: &PathBuf, dep: DepCommands) -> Result<()> {\n1851\t    match dep {\n1852\t        DepCommands::Add {\n1853\t }\n1965\t    Ok(())\n1966\t}\n1967\t\n1968\tfn cmd_label(beads_dir: &PathBuf, label: LabelCommands) -> Result<()> {\n1969\t    match label {\n1970\t        LabelCommands::Add { id, label } => Option<usize>,\n2462\t    since: Option<String>,\n2463\t    actor: Option<String>,\n2464\t    status_changes: bool,\n2465\t    diff: bool,\n2466\t    git: bool,\n2467\t    format: &str,\n2468\t) -> Result<()> {\n2469\t    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n2470\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n2471\t    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n2472\t    let workspace = beads_dir.parent().unwrap_or(beads_dir);\n2473\t\n2474\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n2475\t\n2476\t    // Build filter\n2477\t    let mut filter = crate::log::EventFilter::new();\n2478\t\n2479\t    if let Some(ref issue_id) = id {\n2480\t        filter = filter.with_issue_id(issue_id.clone());\n2481\t    }\n2482\t\n2483\t    if let Some(limit_val) = limit {\n2484\t        filter = filter.with_limit(limit_val);\n2485\t    }\n2486\t\n2487\t    if let Some(ref actor_val) = actor {\n2488\t        filter = filter.with_actor(actor_val.clone());\n2489\t    }\n2490\t\n2491\t    if status_changes {\n2492\t        filter = filter.status_changes_only();\n2493\t    }\n2494\t\n2495\t    if diff {\n2496\t        filter = filter.with_diff();\n2497\t    }\n2498\t\n2499\t    // Parse since date if provided\n2500\t    if let Some(ref since_str) = since {\n2501\t        match    Err(_) => {\n2506\t                return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n2507\t                    \"Invalid --since date format. Use RFC3339 format, e.g., 2026-05-01T00:00:00Z\"\n2508\t                ));\n2509\t            }\n2510\t        }\n2511\t    }\n2512\t\n2513\t    // Query events from SQLite\n2514\t    let crate::git_log::merge_events(sqlite_events, git_events)\n2523\t    } else {\n2524\t        sqlite_events\n2525\t    };\n2526\t\n2527\t    // Apply limit after merging (if limit was set)\n2528\t    let events = if let Some(limit_val) = limit {\n2529\t        if events.len() > limit_val {\n2530\t            // Take the last N events (most recent)\n2531\t            events.into_iter().rev().take(limit_val).rev().collect()\n2532\t        } else {\n2533\t            events\n2534\t        }\n2535\t    } else {\n2536\t        events\n2537\t    };\n2538\t\n2539\t    // Apply actor filter in-memory for git events\n2540\t    let events = if let Some(ref actor_val) = actor {\n2541\t        events\n2542\t            .into_iter()\n2543\t            .filter(|e| e.actor all,\n            format,\n            json,\n        } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_list(\n                &beads_dir, status, type_, assignee, priority, annotation, limit, all, &format,\n            )\n        }\n        Commands::Show { id, format, json } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_show(&beads_dir, &id, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Update {\n            id,\n            title,\n            status,\n            priority,\n            assignee,\n            description,\n            acceptance_criteria,\n            notes,\n            design,\n            due_at,\n        } => cmd_update(\n            &beads_dir,\n            &id,\n            title,\n            status,\n            priority,\n            assignee,\n            description,\n            acceptance_criteria,\n            notes,\n            design,\n            due_at,\n        ),\n        Commands::Close { id, reason } => cmd_close(&beads_dir, &id, &reason),\n        Commands::Reopen { id } => cmd_reopen(&beads_dir, &id),\n        Commands::Delete { id } => cmd_delete(&beads_dir, &id),\n        Commands::Ready {\n            limit,\n            format,\n            json,\n        } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_ready(&beads_dir, limit, &format)\n        }\n        Commands::Claim {\n            assignee,\n            model,\n            harness,\n            harness_version,\n            any,\n            fallback,\n            workspace_paths,\n            dry_run,\n            format,\n            json,\n        } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_claim(\n                &beads_dir,\n                &assignee,\n                model,\n                harness,\n                harness_version,\n                any,\n                fallback.as_deref(),\n                &workspace_paths,\n                dry_run,\n                &format,\n            )\n        }\n        Commands::Sync {\n            flush_only,\n            import_only,\n        } => cmd_sync(&beads_dir, flush_only, import_only),\n        Commands::Doctor {\n            repair,\n            flush_first,\n            force,\n            reclaim_stale,\n            ttl,\n        } => cmd_doctor(&beads_dir, repair, flush_first, force, reclaim_stale, ttl),\n        Commands::CommitCheck => cmd_commit_check(&beads_dir),\n        Commands::Count { status } => cmd_count(&beads_dir, status),\n        Commands::Batch { file, json, stdin } => cmd_batch(&beads_dir, file, json, stdin),\n        Commands::Mitosis {\n            id,\n            children,\n            reason,\n            format,\n        } => cmd_mitosis(&beads_dir, &id, &children, &reason, &format),\n        Commands::Dep(dep) => cmd_dep(&beads_dir, dep),\n        Commands::Label(label) => cmd_label(&beads_dir, label),\n        Commands::Comments(comments) => cmd_comments(&beads_dir, comments),\n        Commands::Search {\n            query,\n            status,\n            type_,\n            assignee,\n            label,\n            priority_min,\n            priority_max,\n            limit,\n            format,\n        } => cmd_search(\n            &beads_dir,\n            query,\n            status,\n            type_,\n            assignee,\n            label,\n            priority_min,\n            priority_max,\n            limit,\n            &format,\n        ),\n        Commands::Stats {\n            by_type,\n            by_priority,\n            by_assignee,\n            by_label,\n            format,\n        } => cmd_stats(\n            &beads_dir,\n            by_type,\n            by_priority,\n            by_assignee,\n            by_label,\n            &format,\n        ),\n        Commands::Schema { target, format } => cmd_schema(&target, &format),\n        Commands::Config(config) => cmd_config(&beads_dir, config),\n        Commands::Velocity {\n            model,\n            harness,\n            format,\n        } => cmd_velocity(&beads_dir, model, harness, &format),\n        Commands::Labels { id, format } => cmd_labels(&beads_dir, &id, &format),\n        Commands::Annotate(annotate) => cmd_annotate(&beads_dir, annotate),\n        Commands::Log {\n            id,\n            limit,\n            since,\n            actor,\n            status_changes,\n            diff,\n            git,\n            format,\n            json,\n        } => {\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n            cmd_log(\n                &beads_dir,\n                id,\n                limit,\n                since,\n                actor,\n                status_changes,\n                diff,\n                git,\n                &format,\n            )\n        }\n        Commands::CriticalPath {\n            id,\n            max_depth,\n            format,\n        } => cmd_critical_path(&beads_dir, &id, max_depth, &format),\n        Commands::Rotate { days, dry_run } => cmd_rotate(&beads_dir, days, dry_run),\n        Commands::Migrate {\n            workspace,\n            from_jsonl,\n            seed_velocity,\n            dry_run,\n            skip_verify,\n        } => cmd_migrate(\n            &beads_dir,\n            workspace,\n            from_jsonl,\n            seed_velocity,\n            dry_run,\n            skip_verify,\n        ),\n    }\n}\n\nfn cmd_init(beads_dir: &PathBuf, prefix: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    std::fs::create_dir_all(beads_dir)?;\n\n    let config_path = beads_dir.join(\"config.yaml\");\n    if !config_path.exists() {\n        let config = format!(\n            r#\"issue_prefixes: [{}]\ndefault_priority: 2\ndefault_type: task\nclaim_ttl_minutes: 30\n\"#,\n            prefix\n        );\n        std::fs::write(&config_path, config)?;\n    }\n\n    let metadata_path = beads_dir.join(\"metadata.json\");\n    if !metadata_path.exists() {\n        let metadata = r#\"{\"database\": \"beads.db\", \"jsonl_export\": \"issues.jsonl\"}\"#;\n        std::fs::write(&metadata_path, metadata)?;\n    }\n\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(\"beads.db\");\n    let _storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let gitignore_path = beads_dir.join(\".gitignore\");\n    if !gitignore_path.exists() {\n        std::fs::write(&gitignore_path, \"beads.db\\nbeads.db-shm\\nbeads.db-wal\\n\")?;\n    }\n\n    println!(\"Initialized bead-forge workspace in {:?}\", beads_dir);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_create(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    title: String,\n    type_: String,\n    priority: i32,\n    description: Option<String>,\n    assignee: Option<String>,\n    labels: Vec<String>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open_with_config(&db_path, &config)?;\n\n    let count = storage.count_issues()?;\n    let prefix = get_default_prefix(&config);\n    let id = crate::id::generate_id(prefix, count);\n\n    let mut issue = Issue::new(id.clone(), title, \".\".to_string());\n    issue.issue_type = IssueType::from_str(type_.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n    issue.priority = Priority(priority);\n    issue.description = description;\n    issue.assignee = assignee;\n    issue.labels = labels;\n\n    storage.create_issue(&issue)?;\n\n    println!(\"{}\", id);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_list(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    status: Option<String>,\n    type_: Option<String>,\n    assignee: Option<String>,\n    priority: Option<i32>,\n    annotation: Option<String>,\n    limit: Option<usize>,\n    all: bool,\n    format: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    // Parse annotation filter (key=value format)\n    let annotation_filter = match annotation {\n        Some(ref ann) => {\n            let parts: Vec<&str> = ann.splitn(2, '=').collect();\n            if parts.len() != 2 {\n                return Err(anyhow!(\"Invalid annotation format. Use key=value\"));\n            }\n            Some((parts[0].to_string(), parts[1].to_string()))\n        }\n        None => None,\n    };\n\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let mut issues = if all {\n        // Include archived beads\n        list_all_with_archives(beads_dir)?\n    } else {\n        // Only from database\n        let mut filter = IssueFilter::default();\n        if let Some(ref s) = status {\n            filter.status = Some(Status::from_str(s.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?);\n        }\n        if let Some(ref t) = type_ {\n            filter.issue_type =\n                Some(IssueType::from_str(t.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?);\n        }\n        filter.assignee = assignee.clone();\n        filter.priority = priority;\n        filter.annotation = annotation_filter.clone();\n        // --limit 0 means unlimited\n        filter.limit = limit.and_then(|l| if l == 0 { None } else { Some(l) });\n        storage.list_issues(&filter)?\n    };\n\n    // Apply additional filters for --all mode (since we're not using DB filters)\n    if all {\n        if let Some(ref s) = status {\n            let status_filter = Status::from_str(s.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n            issues.retain(|i| i.status == status_filter);\n        }\n        if let Some(ref t) = type_ {\n            let type_filter = IssueType::from_str(t.as_str()).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n            issues.retain(|i| i.issue_type == type_filter);\n        }\n        if let Some(ref assignee_val) = assignee {\n            issues.retain(|i| i.assignee.as_deref() == Some(assignee_val));\n        }\n        if let Some(p) = priority {\n            issues.retain(|i| i.priority.0 == p);\n        }\n        if let Some((ref key, ref value)) = annotation_filter {\n            issues.retain(|i| {\n                i.annotations\n                    .get(key)\n                    .map_or(false, |v| v == value)\n            });\n        }\n        // Apply limit\n        if let Some(l) = limit {\n            if l != 0 {\n                issues.truncate(l);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    let output_format = OutputFormat::from_str(format).unwrap_or(OutputFormat::Text);\n    let formatter = get_formatter(output_format);\n    print!(\"{}\", formatter.format_issues(&issues));\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_show(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, format: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let issue = match storage.get_issue(id)? {\n        Some(i) => i,\n        None => {\n            // Search archives\n            find_bead_in_archives(beads_dir, id)?\n                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"Bead not found: {}\", id))?\n        }\n    };\n\n    match format {\n        \"json\" => {\n            // Strip dependencies/comments before serializing: NEEDLE's BrDependency\n            // format ({id, title, status, priority, dependency_type}) differs from\n            // bead-forge's Dependency format ({issue_id, depends_on_id, type, ...}).\n            // NEEDLE has #[serde(default)] on the deps field so empty is fine.\n            let mut out = issue;\n            out.dependencies = vec![];\n            out.comments = vec![];\n            // Wrap in array so NEEDLE's parse_single_bead (Vec<Bead> → first) works.\n            println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string(&vec![out])?);\n        }\n        \"toon\" => {\n            println!(\"ID: {}\", issue.id);\n            println!(\"Title: {}\", issue.title);\n            println!(\"Status: {}\", issue.status);\n            println!(\"Priority: {}\", issue.priority);\n            println!(\"Type: {}\", issue.issue_type);\n            if let Some(desc) = &issue.description {\n                println!(\"Description: {}\", desc);\n            }\n            if let Some(assignee) = &issue.assignee {\n                println!(\"Assignee: {}\", assignee);\n            }\n            if !issue.labels.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"Labels: {}\", issue.labels.join(\", \"));\n            }\n        }\n        _ => {\n            println!(\"ID: {}\", issue.id);\n            println!(\"Title: {}\", issue.title);\n            println!(\"Status: {}\", issue.status);\n            println!(\"Priority: {}\", issue.priority);\n            println!(\"Type: {}\", issue.issue_type);\n            if let Some(desc) = &issue.description {\n                println!(\"Description: {}\", desc);\n            }\n            if let Some(assignee) = &issue.assignee {\n                println!(\"Assignee: {}\", assignee);\n            }\n            if !issue.labels.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"Labels: {}\", issue.labels.join(\", \"));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_update(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    id: &str,\n    title: Option<String>,\n    status: Option<String>,\n    priority: Option<i32>,\n    assignee: Option<String>,\n    description: Option<String>,\n    acceptance_criteria: Option<String>,\n    notes: Option<String>,\n    design: Option<String>,\n    due_at: Option<String>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open_with_config(&db_path, &config)?;\n\n    // Parse due_at if provided\n    let due_at_parsed = match due_at {\n        Some(date_str) => {\n            let dt = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&date_str)\n                .map_err(|_| anyhow!(\"Invalid --due-at format. Use RFC3339 format, e.g., 2025-01-01T00:00:00Z\"))?;\n            Some(dt.with_timezone(&Utc))\n        }\n        None => None,\n    };\n\n    let changes = IssueChanges {\n        title,\n        status: status.map(|s| Status::from_str(&s).ok()).flatten(),\n        priority,\n        assignee,\n        description,\n        acceptance_criteria,\n        notes,\n        design,\n        due_at: due_at_parsed,\n        ..Default::default()\n    };\n\n    storage.update_issue(id, &changes)?;\n    println!(\"Updated bead {}\", id);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_close(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, reason: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    storage.close_issue(id, reason, \"cli\")?;\n    println!(\"Closed bead {}\", id);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_reopen(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let changes = IssueChanges {\n        status: Some(Status::Open),\n        ..Default::default()\n    };\n\n    storage.update_issue(id, &changes)?;\n    println!(\"Reopened bead {}\", id);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_delete(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM issues WHERE id = ?\", [&id])?;\n        Ok(())\n    })?;\n\n    println!(\"Deleted bead {}\", id);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_ready(beads_dir: &PathBuf, limit: usize, format: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    // --limit 0 means unlimited (get_ready_candidates omits LIMIT clause when limit == 0)\n    let candidates = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| get_ready_candidates(tx, limit, None, None))?;\n\n    match format {\n        \"json\" => {\n            for candidate in candidates {\n                println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string(&candidate)?);\n            }\n        }\n        \"toon\" => {\n            for candidate in candidates {\n                println!(\n                    \"{}\",\n                    crate::format::toon::format_ready_bead(\n                        &candidate.id,\n                        &candidate.title,\n                        candidate.priority,\n                        candidate.downstream_impact,\n                        candidate.critical_float,\n                    )\n                );\n            }\n        }\n        _ => {\n            for candidate in candidates {\n                println!(\n                    \"[{}] {} (priority={}, impact={}, float={})\",\n                    candidate.id,\n                    candidate.title,\n                    candidate.priority,\n                    candidate.downstream_impact,\n                    candidate.critical_float\n                );\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_claim(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    assignee: &str,\n    model: Option<String>,\n    harness: Option<String>,\n    harness_version: Option<String>,\n    any: bool,\n    fallback: Option<&str>,\n    workspace_paths: &[PathBuf],\n    dry_run: bool,\n    format: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n    let claim_ttl = config.claim_ttl_minutes;\n\n    // Build worker metadata\n    let worker_metadata = WorkerMetadata {\n        worker_id: assignee.to_string(),\n        model: model.clone(),\n        harness: harness.clone(),\n        harness_version: harness_version.clone(),\n    };\n\n    if dry_run {\n        // Dry run mode - show what would be claimed\n        let candidates: Vec<(PathBuf, crate::claim::ScoredBead)> = if any || fallback == Some(\"any\")\n        {\n            // Multi-workspace dry run\n            let paths = if workspace_paths.is_empty() {\n                // Auto-discover workspaces from current directory\n                find_workspaces(&std::env::current_dir()?)?\n            } else {\n                workspace_paths.to_vec()\n            };\n\n            let mut all_candidates = Vec::new();\n            for path in &paths {\n                let local_beads_dir = path.join(\".beads\");\n                if local_beads_dir.exists() {\n                    let local_metadata = match load_metadata(&local_beads_dir) {\n                        Ok(m) => m,\n                        Err(_) => continue,\n                    };\n                    let local_db_path = local_beads_dir.join(&local_metadata.database);\n                    if let Ok(local_storage) = Storage::open(&local_db_path) {\n                        if let Ok(local_candidates) = local_storage\n                            .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| get_ready_candidates(tx, 1, None, None))\n                        {\n                            for c in local_candidates {\n                                all_candidates.push((path.clone(), c));\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Sort by score and take top 1\n            all_candidates.sort_by(|a, b| {\n                let score_a = (b.1.downstream_impact, b.1.priority, b.1.created_at.clone());\n                let score_b = (a.1.downstream_impact, a.1.priority, a.1.created_at.clone());\n                score_a.cmp(&score_b)\n            });\n            all_candidates.into_iter().take(1).collect()\n        } else {\n            // Single workspace dry run\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            let candidates =\n                storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| get_ready_candidates(tx, 1, None, None))?;\n            candidates\n                .into_iter()\n                .map(|c| (beads_dir.parent().unwrap_or(beads_dir).to_path_buf(), c))\n                .collect()\n        };\n\n        if let Some((path, candidate)) = candidates.first() {\n            match format {\n                \"json\" => {\n                    let output = serde_json::json!({\n                        \"bead_id\": candidate.id,\n                        \"title\": candidate.title,\n                        \"priority\": candidate.priority,\n                        \"downstream_impact\": candidate.downstream_impact,\n                        \"assignee\": assignee,\n                        \"workspace\": path.display().to_string(),\n                        \"dry_run\": true\n                    });\n                    println!(\"{}\", output);\n                }\n                _ => {\n                    println!(\n                        \"{} (priority={}, impact={}, workspace={})\",\n                        candidate.id,\n                        candidate.priority,\n                        candidate.downstream_impact,\n                        path.display()\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        } else if format == \"json\" {\n            println!(\"{{}}\");\n        } else {\n            println!(\"No beads available to claim\");\n        }\n    } else if any {\n        // Claim from any workspace\n        let paths = if workspace_paths.is_empty() {\n            // Auto-discover workspaces from current directory\n            find_workspaces(&std::env::current_dir()?)?\n        } else {\n            workspace_paths.to_vec()\n        };\n\n        let result = claim_any(&paths, assignee, claim_ttl, Some(&worker_metadata))?;\n\n        match result {\n            Some(ClaimResult {\n                bead_id,\n                reclaimed,\n                workspace_path,\n            }) => match format {\n                \"json\" => {\n                    let output = serde_json::json!({\n                        \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                        \"reclaimed\": reclaimed,\n                        \"assignee\": assignee,\n                        \"workspace\": workspace_path.map(|p| p.display().to_string())\n                    });\n                    println!(\"{}\", output);\n                }\n                _ => {\n                    if let Some(path) = workspace_path {\n                        println!(\"{} (workspace: {})\", bead_id, path.display());\n                    } else {\n                        println!(\"{}\", bead_id);\n                    }\n                }\n            },\n            None => {\n                if format == \"json\" {\n                    println!(\"{{}}\");\n                } else {\n                    println!(\"No beads available to claim\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    } else if fallback == Some(\"any\") {\n        // Fallback mode: try current workspace first, then any\n        let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n        let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n        let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n        let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n            claim(tx, assignee, claim_ttl, Utc::now(), Some(&worker_metadata))\n        })?;\n\n        match result {\n            Some(ClaimResult {\n                bead_id, reclaimed, ..\n            }) => match format {\n                \"json\" => {\n                    let output = serde_json::json!({\n                        \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                        \"reclaimed\": reclaimed,\n                        \"assignee\": assignee\n                    });\n                    println!(\"{}\", output);\n                }\n                _ => {\n                    println!(\"{}\", bead_id);\n                }\n            },\n            None => {\n                // Fallback to any workspace\n                let paths = if workspace_paths.is_empty() {\n                    find_workspaces(&std::env::current_dir()?)?\n                } else {\n                    workspace_paths.to_vec()\n                };\n\n                let result = claim_any(&paths, assignee, claim_ttl, Some(&worker_metadata))?;\n\n                match result {\n                    Some(ClaimResult {\n                        bead_id,\n                        reclaimed,\n                        workspace_path,\n                    }) => match format {\n                        \"json\" => {\n                            let output = serde_json::json!({\n                                \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                                \"reclaimed\": reclaimed,\n                                \"assignee\": assignee,\n                                \"workspace\": workspace_path.map(|p| p.display().to_string())\n                            });\n                            println!(\"{}\", output);\n                        }\n                        _ => {\n                            if let Some(path) = workspace_path {\n                                println!(\"{} (workspace: {})\", bead_id, path.display());\n                            } else {\n                                println!(\"{}\", bead_id);\n                            }\n                        }\n                    },\n                    None => {\n                        if format == \"json\" {\n                            println!(\"{{}}\");\n                        } else {\n                            println!(\"No beads available to claim\");\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    } else {\n        // Normal single-workspace claim\n        let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n        let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n        let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n        let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n            claim(tx, assignee, claim_ttl, Utc::now(), Some(&worker_metadata))\n        })?;\n\n        match result {\n            Some(ClaimResult {\n                bead_id, reclaimed, ..\n            }) => match format {\n                \"json\" => {\n                    let output = serde_json::json!({\n                        \"bead_id\": bead_id,\n                        \"reclaimed\": reclaimed,\n                        \"assignee\": assignee\n                    });\n                    println!(\"{}\", output);\n                }\n                _ => {\n                    println!(\"{}\", bead_id);\n                }\n            },\n            None => {\n                if format == \"json\" {\n                    println!(\"{{}}\");\n                } else {\n                    println!(\"No beads available to claim\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_sync(beads_dir: &PathBuf, flush_only: bool, import_only: bool) -> Result<()> {\n    let workspace_dir = beads_dir.parent().unwrap_or(beads_dir);\n\n    if import_only {\n        let result = crate::sync::import(workspace_dir)?;\n        println!(\"Imported {} beads\", result.imported);\n        if result.updated > 0 {\n            println!(\"Updated {} beads\", result.updated);\n        }\n        if result.skipped > 0 {\n            println!(\"Skipped {} unchanged beads\", result.skipped);\n        }\n    } else if flush_only {\n        let count = crate::sync::flush(workspace_dir)?;\n        println!(\"Flushed {} beads to JSONL\", count);\n    } else {\n        let result = crate::sync::sync(workspace_dir)?;\n        println!(\n            \"Synced {} beads from JSONL and flushed {} to JSONL\",\n            result.imported + result.updated,\n            result.exported\n        );\n        if result.updated > 0 {\n            println!(\"Updated {} beads\", result.updated);\n        }\n        if result.skipped > 0 {\n            println!(\"Skipped {} unchanged beads\", result.skipped);\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_doctor(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    repair: bool,\n    flush_first: bool,\n    force: bool,\n    reclaim_stale: bool,\n    ttl: Option<i64>,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n\n    if repair {\n        let workspace_dir = beads_dir.parent().unwrap_or(beads_dir);\n        let imported = crate::doctor::repair(workspace_dir, flush_first, force)?;\n        println!(\n            \"Repaired database: imported {} beads from JSONL\",\n            imported\n        );\n    } else if reclaim_stale {\n        let workspace_dir = beads_dir.parent().unwrap_or(beads_dir);\n        let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n        let ttl_minutes = ttl.unwrap_or(config.claim_ttl_minutes);\n        let reclaimed = crate::doctor::reclaim_stale(workspace_dir, ttl_minutes)?;\n        println!(\"Reclaimed {} stale bead(s)\", reclaimed);\n    } else {\n        // Run health check\n        let workspace_dir = beads_dir.parent().unwrap_or(beads_dir);\n        let result = crate::doctor::check(workspace_dir)?;\n\n        // Report results\n        if result.db_ok && result.jsonl_ok {\n            println!(\"✓ Database integrity: OK\");\n            println!(\"✓ JSONL validity: OK\");\n            println!(\"  Database beads: {}\", result.db_issue_count);\n            println!(\"  JSONL beads: {}\", result.jsonl_line_count);\n\n            let total_drift = result.missing_in_jsonl.len()\n                + result.missing_in_sqlite.len()\n                + result.hash_mismatch.len();\n\n            if total_drift == 0 {\n                println!(\"✓ Consistency: No drift detected\");\n            } else {\n                println!(\"⚠ Consistency: Drift detected\");\n                if !result.missing_in_jsonl.is_empty() {\n                    println!(\"  Missing in JSONL ({}):\", result.missing_in_jsonl.len());\n                    for id in result.missing_in_jsonl.iter().take(10) {\n                        println!(\"    - {}\", id);\n                    }\n                    if result.missing_in_jsonl.len() > 10 {\n                        println!(\"    ... and {} more\", result.missing_in_jsonl.len() - 10);\n                    }\n                }\n                if !result.missing_in_sqlite.is_empty() {\n                    println!(\"  Missing in SQLite ({}):\", result.missing_in_sqlite.len());\n                    for id in result.missing_in_sqlite.iter().take(10) {\n                        println!(\"    - {}\", id);\n                    }\n                    if result.missing_in_sqlite.len() > 10 {\n                        println!(\"    ... and {} more\", result.missing_in_sqlite.len() - 10);\n                    }\n                }\n                if !result.hash_mismatch.is_empty() {\n                    println!(\"  Hash mismatch ({}):\", result.hash_mismatch.len());\n                    for id in result.hash_mismatch.iter().take(10) {\n                        println!(\"    - {}\", id);\n                    }\n                    if result.hash_mismatch.len() > 10 {\n                        println!(\"    ... and {} more\", result.hash_mismatch.len() - 10);\n                    }\n                }\n                println!();\n                println!(\"Run 'bf doctor --repair' to rebuild SQLite from JSONL\");\n            }\n\n            // Report unflushed beads (if any)\n            if result.unflushed_count > 0 {\n                println!(\"⚠ Unflushed beads: {}\", result.unflushed_count);\n                println!(\"  Run 'bf sync --flush-only' before repair to avoid data loss\");\n            }\n        } else {\n            if !result.db_ok {\n                println!(\"✗ Database integrity: FAILED\");\n            }\n            if !result.jsonl_ok {\n                println!(\"✗ JSONL validity: FAILED\");\n            }\n            for issue in &result.issues {\n                eprintln!(\"  {}\", issue);\n            }\n            if !result.jsonl_ok || !result.db_ok {\n                println!();\n                println!(\"Run 'bf doctor --repair' to rebuild SQLite from JSONL\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_commit_check(beads_dir: &PathBuf) -> Result<()> {\n    let result = scan_staged_beads(beads_dir)?;\n\n    if result.secrets_found.is_empty() {\n        // Clean - no output on success (standard for pre-commit hooks)\n        std::process::exit(0);\n    }\n\n    // Secrets found - print details and exit 1\n    eprintln!(\"{}\", format_scan_results(&result));\n    std::process::exit(1);\n}\n\nfn cmd_count(beads_dir: &PathBuf, status: Option<String>) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let count = if let Some(s) = status {\n        let filter = IssueFilter {\n            status: Some(Status::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?),\n            ..Default::default()\n        };\n        storage.list_issues(&filter)?.len()\n    } else {\n        storage.count_issues()?\n    };\n\n    println!(\"{}\", count);\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_batch(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    file: Option<PathBuf>,\n    json: Option<String>,\n    stdin: bool,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open_with_config(&db_path, &config)?;\n\n    let ops: Vec<BatchOp> = if let Some(json_str) = json {\n        serde_json::from_str(&json_str)?\n    } else if let Some(file_path) = file {\n        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&file_path)?;\n        serde_json::from_str(&content)?\n    } else if stdin {\n        parse_stdin()?\n    } else {\n        return Err(anyhow!(\"Must provide --file, --json, or --stdin\"));\n    };\n\n    let results = execute_batch(&storage, ops, beads_dir)?;\n\n    // Print results\n    for result in results {\n        if result.status == \"ok\" {\n            if let Some(id) = result.id {\n                println!(\"[op {}] ok: {}\", result.op, id);\n            } else {\n                println!(\"[op {}] ok\", result.op);\n            }\n        } else {\n            eprintln!(\n                \"[op {}] error: {}\",\n                result.op,\n                result.error.unwrap_or_default()\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_mitosis(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    id: &str,\n    children: &str,\n    reason: &str,\n    format: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open_with_config(&db_path, &config)?;\n\n    // Parse children as JSON array of {title, type, priority} or {title, type, priority, description, assignee, labels}\n    let children_defs: Vec<MitosisChild> = serde_json::from_str(children)?;\n\n    // Build the batch operations\n    let ops = mitosis_ex(id, children_defs, Some(reason.to_string()))?;\n\n    // Execute atomically\n    let results = execute_batch(&storage, ops, beads_dir)?;\n\n    match format {\n        \"json\" => {\n            println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&results)?);\n        }\n        _ => {\n            // Print child IDs that were created\n            for result in &results {\n                if let Some(child_id) = &result.id {\n                    println!(\"Created child: {}\", child_id);\n                }\n            }\n            println!(\n                \"Parent bead {} closed with {} children\",\n                id,\n                results.len() - 2\n            ); // -2 for close + last dep\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Format and print a dependency tree.\nfn print_dep_tree(nodes: &[crate::storage::DepTreeNode], _storage: &Storage) -> Result<()> {\n    if nodes.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"  (no dependencies)\");\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    // Status indicators: ●=open, ◐=in_progress, ○=closed/blocked/deferred, ⊘=tombstone\n    let status_symbol = |status: &str| -> char {\n        match status {\n            \"open\" => '●',\n            \"in_progress\" => '◐',\n            \"closed\" => '○',\n            \"blocked\" => '◌',\n            \"deferred\" => '○',\n            \"tombstone\" => '⊘',\n            _ => '○',\n        }\n    };\n\n    for (i, node) in nodes.iter().enumerate() {\n        let is_cycle = node.path.contains(\"[CYCLE]\");\n\n        // Build tree prefix with proper branching\n        let mut prefix = String::new();\n        if node.depth > 0 {\n            for d in 0..node.depth as usize {\n                if d < node.depth as usize - 1 {\n                    prefix.push_str(\"│   \");\n                } else {\n                    // Check if this is the last node at this depth\n                    let is_last = nodes\n                        .iter()\n                        .skip(i + 1)\n                        .all(|n| n.depth < node.depth || (n.depth == node.depth && n.id > node.id));\n                    if is_last {\n                        prefix.push_str(\"└── \");\n                    } else {\n                        prefix.push_str(\"├── \");\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Truncate title if too long\n        let title = if node.title.len() > 60 {\n            format!(\"{}...\", &node.title[..57])\n        } else {\n            node.title.clone()\n        };\n\n        let cycle_mark = if is_cycle { \" [CYCLE]\" } else { \"\" };\n        let dep_type_str = node.dep_type.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"blocks\");\n\n        println!(\n            \"{}[{}] {} {} (P{}, {}){}\",\n            prefix,\n            node.id,\n            status_symbol(&node.status),\n            title,\n            node.priority,\n            dep_type_str,\n            cycle_mark\n        );\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_dep(beads_dir: &PathBuf, dep: DepCommands) -> Result<()> {\n    match dep {\n        DepCommands::Add {\n            issue,\n            depends_on,\n            type_,\n        } => {\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            let dep_type =\n                crate::model::DependencyType::from_str(&type_).map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?;\n            storage.add_dependency(&issue, &depends_on, &dep_type, \"cli\")?;\n            println!(\n                \"Added dependency: {} depends on {} ({})\",\n                issue, depends_on, type_\n            );\n        }\n        DepCommands::Remove { issue, depends_on } => {\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            storage.remove_dependency(&issue, &depends_on)?;\n            println!(\"Removed dependency: {} -> {}\", issue, depends_on);\n        }\n        DepCommands::List { id } => {\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            let deps = storage.get_dependencies(&id)?;\n            if deps.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"No dependencies found for {}\", id);\n            } else {\n                for dep in deps {\n                    println!(\n                        \"  {} depends on {} ({})\",\n                        dep.issue_id, dep.depends_on_id, dep.dep_type\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        DepCommands::Tree {\n            id,\n            direction,\n            max_depth,\n            format,\n            json,\n        } => {\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            let format = if json { \"json\".to_string() } else { format };\n\n            // Validate direction\n            let direction = match direction.as_str() {\n                \"down\" | \"up\" | \"both\" => direction.as_str(),\n                _ => {\n                    return Err(anyhow!(\n                        \"Invalid direction: {}. Use 'down', 'up', or 'both'\",\n                        direction\n                    ))\n                }\n            };\n\n            if format == \"json\" {\n                // JSON output format\n                if direction == \"both\" {\n                    let down_nodes = storage.get_dep_tree(&id, \"down\", max_depth)?;\n                    let up_nodes = storage.get_dep_tree(&id, \"up\", max_depth)?;\n                    let output = serde_json::json!({\n                        \"root_id\": id,\n                        \"direction\": direction,\n                        \"max_depth\": max_depth,\n                        \"downward\": down_nodes,\n                        \"upward\": up_nodes\n                    });\n                    println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&output)?);\n                } else {\n                    let nodes = storage.get_dep_tree(&id, direction, max_depth)?;\n                    let output = serde_json::json!({\n                        \"root_id\": id,\n                        \"direction\": direction,\n                        \"max_depth\": max_depth,\n                        \"nodes\": nodes\n                    });\n                    println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&output)?);\n                }\n            } else {\n                // Text output format\n                if direction == \"both\" {\n                    // Show both directions separately\n                    println!(\n                        \"Dependency tree for {} (downward - what this depends on):\\n\",\n                        id\n                    );\n                    let down_nodes = storage.get_dep_tree(&id, \"down\", max_depth)?;\n                    print_dep_tree(&down_nodes, &storage)?;\n\n                    if !down_nodes.is_empty() {\n                        println!();\n                    }\n\n                    println!(\n                        \"Reverse dependency tree for {} (upward - what depends on this):\\n\",\n                        id\n                    );\n                    let up_nodes = storage.get_dep_tree(&id, \"up\", max_depth)?;\n                    print_dep_tree(&up_nodes, &storage)?;\n                } else {\n                    let nodes = storage.get_dep_tree(&id, direction, max_depth)?;\n                    print_dep_tree(&nodes, &storage)?;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_label(beads_dir: &PathBuf, label: LabelCommands) -> Result<()> {\n    match label {\n        LabelCommands::Add { id, label } => {\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            for l in label {\n                storage.add_label(&id, &l)?;\n                println!(\"Added label '{}' to {}\", l, id);\n            }\n        }\n        LabelCommands::Remove { id, label } => {\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            for l in label {\n                storage.remove_label(&id, &l)?;\n                println!(\"Removed label '{}' from {}\", l, id);\n            }\n        }\n        LabelCommands::List { id } => {\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            if let Some(id) = id {\n                let labels = storage.get_labels(&id)?;\n                println!(\"Labels for {}:\", id);\n                for label in labels {\n                    println!(\"  {}\", label);\n                }\n            } else {\n                let labels = storage.list_all_labels()?;\n                println!(\"All labels:\");\n                for (label, count) in labels {\n                    println!(\"  {} ({})\", label, count);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_labels(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, format: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n    let labels = storage.get_labels(id)?;\n    if format == \"json\" {\n        println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&labels)?);\n    } else {\n        for label in CommentsCommands::Add { id, text } => {\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = {}\", comment_id, id);\n        }\n        CommentsCommands::List { id } => {\n            let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n           } else {\n                for comment in comments {\n                    println!(\"  [{}] {}: {}\", comment.id, comment.author, comment.body);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_search(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    query: Option<String>,\n    status: Vec<String>,\n    type_: Vec<String>,\n    assignee: Option<String>,\n    label: Vec<String>,\n    priority_min: Option<i32>,\n    priority_max: Option<i32>,\n    limit: usize,\n    format: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let statuses: Vec<Status> = status\n        .iter()\n        .filter_map(|s| Status::from_str(s).ok())\n        .collect();\n    let types: Vec<IssueType> = type_\n        .iter()\n        .filter_map(|t| IssueType::from_str(t).ok())\n        .collect();\n\n    let issues = storage.search_issues(\n        query.as_deref(),\n        &statuses,\n        &types,\n        assignee.as_deref(),\n        &label,\n        priority_min,\n        priority_max,\n        limit,\n    )?;\n\n    let output_format = cmd_stats(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    by_type: bool,\n    by_priority: bool,\n    by_assignee: bool,\n    by_label: bool,\n    format: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n    let stats = storage.get_stats()?;\n\n    match format {\n        \"json\" => {\n            println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&stats)?);\n        }\n        _ => {\n            println!(\"Total beads: {}\", stats.total);\n            println!(\"  Open: {}\", stats.open);\n            println!(\"  In Progress: {}\", stats.in_progress);\n            println!(\"  Closed: {}\", stats.closed);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if by_type {\n        let by_type_stats = storage.get_stats_by_type()?;\n        println!(\"\\nBy type:\");\n        for (issue_type, count) in by_type_stats {\n            println!(\"  {} ({})\", issue_type, count);\n        }\n    }\n    if by_priority {\n        let by_priority_stats = storage.get_stats_by_priority()?;\n        println!(\"\\nBy priority:\");\n        for (priority, count) in by_priority_stats {\n            println!(\"  P{} ({})\", priority, count);\n        }\n    }\n    if by_assignee {\n        let by_assignee_stats = storage.get_stats_by_assignee()?;\n        println!(\"\\nBy assignee:\");\n        if by_assignee_stats.is_empty() {\n            println!(\"  (no assigned beads)\");\n        } else {\n            for (assignee, count) in by_assignee_stats {\n                println!(\"  {} ({})\", assignee.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"None\"), count);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    if by_label {\n        let labels = storage.list_all_labels()?;\n        println!(\"\\nBy label:\");\n        for (label, count) in labels {\n            println!(\"  {} ({})\", label, count);\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_schema(target: &str, format: &str) -> Result<()> {\n    match target {\n        \"all\" => {\n            // Print SQLite schema DDL for all bf tables\n            match format {\n                \"json\" => {\n                    let output = serde_json::json!({\n                        \"schema\": crate::storage::schema::SCHEMA_SQL\n                    });\n                    println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&output)?);\n                }\n                _ => {\n                    println!(\"{}\", crate::storage::schema::SCHEMA_SQL);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        bead_id => {\n            // Print that bead's full JSON representation including annotations\n            let current_dir = std::env::current_dir()?;\n            let beads_dir = crate::config::find_beads_dir(&current_dir)\n                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found\"))?;\n            let metadata = crate::config::load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = crate::storage::Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n            let mut issue = match storage.get_issue(bead_id)? {\n                Some(i) => i,\n                None => {\n                    // Search archives\n                    crate::rotate::find_bead_in_archives(&beads_dir, bead_id)?\n                        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"Bead not found: {}\", bead_id))?\n                }\n            };\n\n            // Load annotations for this bead\n            issue.annotations = storage.get_annotations(bead_id)?;\n\n            match format {\n                \"json\" => {\n                    println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&issue)?);\n                }\n                _ => {\n                    println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&issue)?);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_config(beads_dir: &PathBuf, config: ConfigCommands) -> Result<()> {\n    match config {\n        ConfigCommands::List => {\n            let cfg = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n            println!(\"Config:\");\n            println!(\"  issue_prefixes: {:?}\", cfg.issue_prefixes);\n            println!(\"  default_priority: {}\", cfg.default_priority);\n            println!(\"  default_type: {}\", cfg.default_type);\n            println!(\"  claim_ttl_minutes: {}\", cfg.claim_ttl_minutes);\n        }\n        ConfigCommands::Get { key } => {\n            let cfg = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n            // Parse key with dot notation support (e.g., scoring.priority_weight)\n            let parts: Vec<&str> = key.split('.').collect();\n            let value = match parts.as_slice() {\n                [\"issue_prefixes\"] => format!(\"{:?}\", cfg.issue_prefixes),\n                [\"default_priority\"] => cfg.default_priority.to_string(),\n                [\"default_type\"] => cfg.default_type.clone(),\n                [\"claim_ttl_minutes\"] => cfg.claim_ttl_minutes.to_string(),\n                [\"scoring\", \"priority_weight\"] => cfg.scoring.priority_weight.to_string(),\n                [\"scoring\", \"blockers_weight\"] => cfg.scoring.blockers_weight.to_string(),\n                [\"scoring\", \"age_weight\"] => cfg.scoring.age_weight.to_string(),\n                [\"scoring\", \"labels_weight\"] => cfg.scoring.labels_weight.to_string(),\n                [\"scoring\", \"max_age_hours\"] => cfg.scoring.max_age_hours.to_string(),\n                [\"scoring\", \"max_blockers\"] => cfg.scoring.max_blockers.to_string(),\n                [\"rotate\", \"rotate_age_days\"] => cfg.rotate.rotate_age_days.to_string(),\n                [\"rotate\", \"rotate_max_size_mb\"] => cfg.rotate.rotate_max_size_mb.to_string(),\n                [\"rotate\", \"rotate_max_archives\"] => cfg.rotate.rotate_max_archives.to_string(),\n                [\"secret_protection\", \"enabled\"] => cfg.secret_protection.enabled.to_string(),\n                _ => return Err(anyhow!(\"Unknown config key: {}\", key)),\n            };\n            println!(\"{}\", value);\n        }\n        ConfigCommands::Set { key, value } => {\n            use crate::config::save_config;\n            let mut cfg = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n\n            // Parse key with dot notation support (e.g., scoring.priority_weight)\n            let parts: Vec<&str> = key.split('.').collect();\n            let result = match parts.as_slice() {\n                [\"issue_prefixes\"] => {\n                    // Parse as comma-separated list: \"bf,nf,xf\"\n                    cfg.issue_prefixes = value\n                        .split(',')\n                        .map(|s| s.trim().to_string())\n                        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())\n                        .collect();\n                    Ok(())\n                }\n                [\"default_priority\"] => {\n                    cfg.default_priority = value\n                        .parse()\n                        .map_err(|_| anyhow!(\"Invalid priority: {}. Must be 0-4\", value))?;\n                    Ok(())\n                }\n                [\"default_type\"] => {\n                    cfg.default_type = value.clone();\n                    Ok(())\n                }\n                [\"claim_ttl_minutes\"] => {\n                    cfg.claim_ttl_minutes = value\n                        .parse()\n                        .map_err(|_| anyhow!(\"Invalid TTL minutes: {}. Must be an integer\", value))?;\n                    Ok(())\n                }\n                [\"scoring\", \"priority_weight\"] => {\n                    cfg.scoring.priority_weight = value\n                        .parse()\n                        .map_err(|_| anyhow!(\"Invalid priority_weight: {}. Must be a number\", value))?;\n                    Ok(())\n                }\n                [\"scoring\", \"blockers_weight\"] => {\n                    cfg.scoring.blockers_weight = value\n                        .parse()\n                        .map_err(|_| anyhow!(\"Invalid blockers_weight: {}. Must be a number\", value))?;\n                    Ok(())\n                }\n                [\"scoring\", \"age_weight\"] => {\n                    cfg.scoring.age_weight = value\n                        .parse()\n                        .map_err(|_| anyhow!(\"Invalid age_weight: {}.      println!(\"{}\", config_path.display());\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_velocity(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    model: Option<String>,\n    harness: Option<String>,\n    format: println!(\"{}\", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&stats)?);\n        }\n        \"toon\" => {\n            for stat in stats {\n                println!(\"Model: {}\", stat.model);\n                println!(\"Harness: {}\", stat.harness);\n                println!(\"Type: {}\", stat.issue_type);\n                println!(\"Samples: {}\", stat.sample_count);\n                if let Some(p50) = stat.p50_seconds {\n                    println!(\"P50: {}s\", p50);\n                }\n                if let Some(p90) = stat.p90_seconds {\n                    println!(\"P90: {}s\", p90);\n                }\n                if let Some(avg) = stat.avg_seconds {\n                    println!(\"Avg: {:.1}s\", avg);\n                }\n                println!();\n            }\n        }\n        _ => {\n            if stats.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"No velocity statistics available yet.\");\n                println!(\"Velocity data accumulates as beads are claimed and closed.\");\n            } else {\n                println!(\"Velocity Statistics:\");\n                println!();\n                println!(\n                    \"{:<20} {:<15} {:<10} {:<8} {:<8} {:<8} {:<8}\",\n                    \"Model\", \"Harness\", \"Type\", \"Samples\", \"P50(s)\", \"P90(s)\", \"Avg(s)\"\n                );\n                println!(\"{}\", \"-\".repeat(85));\n stat.harness,\n                        stat.issue_type,\n                        stat.sample_count,\n                        stat.p50_seconds\n                            .map(|s| s.to_string())\n                            .unwrap_or_else(|| \"-\".to_string()),\n                        stat.p90_seconds\n                            .map(|s| s.to_string())\n                            .unwrap_or_else(|| \"-\".to_string()),\n                        stat.avg_seconds.unwrap_or(0.0),\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_annotate(beads_dir: &PathBuf, annotate: AnnotateCommands) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    match annotate {\n        AnnotateCommands::Set { id, key, value } => {\n            storage.set_annotation(&id, &key, &value)?;\n            println!(\"Set annotation '{}' on {}\", key, id);\n        }\n        AnnotateCommands::Get { id, key } => {\n            let annotations = storage.get_annotations(&id)?;\n            if let Some(value) = annotations.get(&key) {\n                println!(\"{}\", value);\n            } else {\n                println!(\"Annotation '{}' not found on {}\", key, id);\n            }\n        }\n        AnnotateCommands::Remove { id, key } => {\n            storage.remove_annotation(&id, &key)?;\n            println!(\"Removed annotation '{}' from {}\", key, id);\n        }\n        AnnotateCommands::List { id } => {\n            let annotations = storage.get_annotations(&id)?;\n            if annotations.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"No annotations for {}\", id);\n            } else {\n                println!(\"Annotations for {}:\", id);\n                for (key, value) in annotations {\n                    println!(\"  {}: {}\", key, value);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        AnnotateCommands::Clear { id } => {\n            storage.clear_annotations(&id)?;\n            println!(\"Cleared all annotations from {}\", id);\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_log(\n    beads_dir: &PathBuf,\n    id: Option<String>,\n    limit: Option<usize>,\n    since: Option<String>,\n    actor: Option<String>,\n    status_changes: bool,\n    diff: bool,\n    git: bool,\n    format: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    let metadata = load_metadata(beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n    let workspace = beads_dir.parent().unwrap_or(beads_dir);\n\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    // Build filter\n    let mut filter = crate::log::EventFilter::new();\n\n    if let Some(ref issue_id) = id {\n        filter = filter.with_issue_id(issue_id.clone());\n    }\n\n    if let Some(limit_val) = limit {\n        filter = filter.with_limit(limit_val);\n    }\n\n    if let Some(ref actor_val) = actor {\n        filter = filter.with_actor(actor_val.clone());\n    }\n\n    if status_changes {\n        filter = filter.status_changes_only();\n    }\n\n    if diff {\n        filter = filter.with_diff();\n    }\n\n    // Parse since date if provided\n    if let Some(ref since_str) = since {\n        match chrono::DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(since_str) {\n            Ok(dt) => {\n                filter = filter.with_since(dt.with_timezone(&chrono::Utc));\n            }\n            Err(_) => {\n                return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n                    \"Invalid --since date format. Use RFC3339 format, e.g., 2026-05-01T00:00:00Z\"\n                ));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Query events from SQLite\n    let sqlite_events = crate::log::query_events(&storage, &filter)?;\n\n    // If --git flag is set, also query git history\n    let events = if git {\n        let git_events =\n            crate::git_log::reconstruct_events_from_git(workspace, &jsonl_path, id.as_deref())?;\n\n        // Merge git events with SQLite events\n        crate::git_log::merge_events(sqlite_events, git_events)\n    } else {\n        sqlite_events\n    };\n\n    // Apply limit after merging (if limit was set)\n    let events = if let Some(limit_val) = limit {\n        if events.len() > limit_val {\n            // Take the last N events (most recent)\n            events.into_iter().rev().take(limit_val).rev().collect()\n        } else {\n            events\n        }\n    } else {\n        events\n    };\n\n    // Apply actor filter in-memory for git events\n    let events = if let Some(ref actor_val) = actor {\n        events\n            .into_iter()\n            .filter(|e| e.actor == *actor_val)\n            .collect()\n    } else {\n        events\n    };\n\n    // Apply status_changes_only filter in-memory\n    let events = if status_changes {\n        events\n            .into_iter()\n            .filter(|e| {\n                matches!(\n                    e.event_type,\n                    crate::model::EventType::StatusChanged\n                        | crate::model::EventType::Closed\n                        | crate::model::EventType::Reopened\n                        | crate::model::EventType::PriorityChanged\n                        | crate::model::EventType::AssigneeChanged\n                )\n            })\n            .collect()\n    } else {\n        events\n    };\n\n    match format {\n        \"json\" => {\n            println!(\"{}\", crate::log::format_events_json(&events)?);\n        }\n        \"toon\" => {\n            for event in &events {\n                println!(\"{}\", crate::log::format_event_toon(event));\n            }\n        }\n        _ => {\n            if events.is_empty() {\n                println!(\"No events found\");\n            } else {\n                for event in &events {\n                    println!(\"{}\", crate::log::format_event_text(event, diff));\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_critical_path(beads_dir: &PathBuf, id: &str, _max_depth: usize, format:              println!(\n                    \"Minimum remaining time: {} bead-completions on critical path\",\n                    result.min_remaining\n                );\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn cmd_rotate(beads_dir: &PathBuf, days: u64, dry_run: bool) -> Result<()> {\n    let config = load_config(beads_dir)?;\n\n    let mut options =
